- **Workflow Reviewer**: Automated agent that reviews GitHub Actions workflow runs, identifies failures, and creates pull requests with tested and functional fixes. ([bamr87/bamr87](https://github.com/bamr87/bamr87/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/workflow-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **Workflow Support**: Says what state a run is actually in and what will move it. Reads the run record, the gate result and the failing task's output, and answers with the act and the one task that takes it, or says plainly that nothing here fixes it. Owns no stage and passes no gate. Takes no document as its input; writes no document; reaches nothing outside the repository; tools: readFile, listDirectory. Trigger phrases: what state is this run in, why did that task fail, what moves this stage, I cannot find a route, the task failed and did not say why. ([TominoHav/SPOCK-Workflow-1.0](https://github.com/TominoHav/SPOCK-Workflow-1.0/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/workflow-support.agent.md))
- **Workflow Verifier**: Hidden check tier: verifies a plain work result, checks an integrated code batch (verify and review together), runs the dev baseline, refutes design proposals and review findings, and does the final critic pass, as a workflow node. Answers with one verdict document; its `fail` makes the workflow loop repeat the work, and its `stalled` ends a loop whose last pass fixed nothing. ([iii-hq/workers](https://github.com/iii-hq/workers/blob/HEAD/kits/workflow/agents/workflow-verifier.md) · ★114)
- **Workflow Worker**: Hidden leaf: does one plain work card (research, reading, analysis) as a workflow node and answers with one result document. Never edits ticket fields. Base of the work tiers (`workflow-worker`, `workflow-impl`). ([iii-hq/workers](https://github.com/iii-hq/workers/blob/HEAD/kits/workflow/agents/workflow-worker.md) · ★114)

- **worklog**: 決定の記録: `docs/adr/998-worklog-vault-daily-note.md` 実体: `crates/worklog`(CLI と SessionStart hook) 配備: 個人の機体(vega)は `home/hosts/vega/obsidian.nix`(CLI `~/.local/bin/worklog` と vault のマーカー)。 会社の機体(arcturus)は、`home/hosts/arcturus.nix` が vault の明示初期化(`dotfiles.obsidian.initializeVault`)を有効にし、 会社用 vault の場所とマーカーは private 側が宣言する(`docs/adr/1136-wrapup-inbox-to-vault.md` D3。2026-11-13 に arcturus で、 マーカーが会社用 ([tarotene/dotfiles](https://github.com/tarotene/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/worklog.md) · ★1)
